			/* Hilary Duff */
.postid-293049 .curated-posts .story-link:nth-of-type(3),
.postid-293049 .curated-posts .story-link:last-of-type {
	display: none;
}

.has-contrast-background-color .story-link {
	--wp--custom--scheme--base--text-subdued: var(--wp--custom--palette--static-gray-100);
}

.overlay-header .wp-block-cover__inner-container .wp-element-caption {
	left: 0;
	text-align: center;
	width: 100%;
}

figure.wp-block-gallery.is-style-emphasis-top :nth-child(-n + 2) {
  flex-basis: 40%;
}

.has-underline-hover:is(:hover, :focus), .has-child-underline-hover > *:is(:hover, :focus) {
	text-decoration: underline !important;
}
.has-underline-hover, .has-child-underline-hover > * {
	text-decoration: none !important;
}
@media (max-width: 782px) {
    .is-responsive-grid.is-col-primary-side {
        gap: 1rem 0 !important;
    }
}
@media (min-width: 783px) {
    .is-responsive-grid.is-col-primary-side {
        gap: 60px 21px !important;
    }

    .is-col-primary-side {
        grid-template-columns: 1fr  292px;
    }

    .is-col-primary-side > :first-child {
        margin-right: -1rem !important;
        width: 100%;
        max-width: 100%;
    }
	
	.is-col-primary-side.is-fit-narrow {
		grid-template-columns: fit-content(528px) 292px;
	}
}

/* Moving Forward Together */ .page-id-225630 .page__header {
	margin-bottom: 0;
}

.page-id-225630 .page__header .wrapper {
	padding-bottom: 0;
}

.live-updates iframe {
    max-width: 100%;
    min-width: 0 !important;
    margin-inline: auto !important;
    display: block;
}


.live-updates .twitter-tweet {
    margin-inline: auto;
}

.entry__content .transition.alignfull {
    margin-inline: 0;
}

.transition__frame {
    display: flex;
    justify-content: center;
    align-items: center;
}

article.magazine-article .transition .transition__frame h2.wp-block-heading {
    width: unset !important
}


@media (min-width: 600px) {
    .single-magazine-article .nunews-athlete__profile figure {
        margin-inline: unset;
        width: unset;
    }
}


.single-newspost .entry-content p a {
    text-decoration: underline
}

.single-newspost .post-meta__body {
    display: flex;
    flex-direction: column;
    justify-content: center;
}

.home .seen-around-card .subtitle {
    max-width: unset;
}

@media (min-width: 600.1px) {
    .single-magazine-article .wp-block-separator {
        width: 72% !important;
    }

    hr.wp-block-separator {
        border-style: solid !important;
        border-color: var(--wp--custom--color--border);
        border-top-width: var(--wp--custom--border--thin, 1px);
        border-bottom-width: 0;
    }

    .wp-block-separator:not(.is-style-wide) {
        width: 130px;
    }

    .wp-block-separator.alignfull {
        width: 100vw !important;
        max-width: unset;
    }

    .featured-posts #post-detail-1 img {
        object-position: left;
    }

    .featured-posts #post-detail-2 img {
        object-position: left;
    }

    .featured-posts #post-detail-3 img {
        object-position: left;
    }

    .wp-block-image:where(.alignright, .alignleft) img {
        width: 100%;
        height: auto;
    }

    .post-173540 .wp-block-nunews-slick-slider {
        background: #efefef;
        margin: 64px auto;
        max-width: var(--wp--custom--layout--content-size);
    }

    .post-173540 .wp-block-nunews-slick-slide {
        width: 312px;
    }

    @media (max-width: 600px) {
        .viewportfull {
            height: 100vh;
            width: 100vw;
            position: absolute;
            inset: 0 0 0 -32px;
            max-width: 100vw;
        }

        .viewportfull > img {
            object-fit: cover;
            height: 100% !important;
            width: 100% !important;
        }
    }.headline + .entry__meta {
         max-width: var(--wp--custom--layout--content-size);
         margin-inline: auto;
     }

    .wp-block-embed.alignwide.is-type-video.is-provider-youtube.wp-block-embed-youtube.wp-embed-aspect-16-9.wp-has-aspect-ratio .article__video-tease.article__video-tease--small.show-video-embed .article__video-container {
        text-align: center;
    }

    .post-178917 .entry-header__media {
        display: block;
    }

    .single-magazine-article :where(.wp-block-image, .wp-block-heading):is(.alignwide) {
        margin-inline: auto;
        max-width: var(--wp--custom--layout--wide-size);
    }

    .post-181341.type-magazine-article .nunews-scroll-panel__pinned {
        height: fit-content !important;
    }

    @media (max-width: 600px) {
        .featured-posts__details {
            --wp--custom--color--emphasize: #000 !important;
            --wp--custom--color--foreground: #000 !important;
        }
    }
    @media (min-width: 1441px) {
        .featured-posts__title .title {
            padding-inline: 36px !important;
        }
    }article.magazine-article h2.wp-block-heading {
         width: 90% !important;
     }

    .nunews-archive .wp-block-post {
        z-index: initial;
    }

    .aligncenter {
        display: flex;
        justify-content: center;
        flex-wrap: wrap;
    }

    .is-provider-ted .article__embed {
        margin: 60px auto !important;
    }

    .nunews-archive .wp-block-post-excerpt__more-link svg {
        width: 16px;
        margin-left: 4px
    }

    .tax-campus.term-oakland a:link, .tax-campus.term-oakland a:visited {
        text-decoration: underline
    }

    .tax-campus.term-oakland a:link:hover, .tax-campus.term-oakland a:visited:hover {
        text-decoration: none;
        color: #005185
    }
}

.featured-posts__details .post-details__link {
    color: transparent !important;
    display: block;
    inset: 0;
    position: absolute;
    z-index: 2;
    height: calc(100% + 5rem);
}

.page-id-210009 .intro {
    box-sizing: border-box;
    max-width: var(--wp--custom--layout--content-size);
    margin-inline: auto;
}

@media screen and (max-width: 700px) {
    .page-id-210009 .page__header .title {
        text-align: left;
    }
}

.wp-block-nunews-transition-frame {
    z-index: 1;
}

.magazine-article .collateral.alignwide .twitter-tweet.twitter-tweet-rendered {
    margin-right: auto;
    margin-left: auto;
}


/* pin and scroll safari patch */
.pin-and-scroll .wp-block-media-text .wp-block-media-text__media {
    z-index: -1;
}

@media (max-width: 781.9px) {
    .wp-block-heading.has-xx-large-font-size {
        --wp--preset--font-size--xx-large: 2.1rem;
    }

    .story-highlight__headline {
        --wp--custom--font-size--xxx-large: 2.1rem !important;
    }
}

.magazine-article .entry__content > .is-style-dek {
    max-width: calc(var(--wp--custom--layout--wide-size) - 40px) !important;
}

/** Commencement articles */
@media screen and (max-width: 959px) {
    .has-hidden-mobile-images .post-thumbnail {
        display: none;
    }
}

@media screen and (min-width: 960px) {
    #highlights .featured-articles.has-spread-view {
        max-width: var(--wp--custom--layout--extra-extra-wide-size) !important;
    }

    #highlights .featured-articles.has-spread-view > div:first-of-type {
        display: grid;
        grid-column-gap: calc(2rem + 1px);
        grid-row-gap: 8px;
        grid-template-columns:repeat(15, 1fr);
        grid-template-rows: repeat(2, 1fr);
    }

    #highlights .featured-articles.has-spread-view > div:first-of-type > article {
        grid-column: span 6;
        width: 100%;

    }

    #highlights .featured-articles.has-spread-view > div:first-of-type > article .entry-title {
        font-size: var(--wp--custom--font-size--large) !important;
    }

    #highlights .featured-articles.has-spread-view > div:first-of-type > article:first-of-type {
        grid-column: 1/ span 11;
        grid-row: 1/  -1;
    }

    #highlights .featured-articles.has-spread-view > div:first-of-type > article:first-of-type .entry-title {
        font-size: var(--wp--custom--font-size--x-large) !important;

    }

    #highlights .featured-articles.has-spread-view > div:first-of-type > article:nth-of-type(2) {
        grid-row: 1;
        grid-column: 12/ span 4
    }

    #highlights .featured-articles.has-spread-view > div:first-of-type > article:nth-of-type(3) {
        grid-row: 2;
        grid-column: 12 / span 4
    }
}

/** commencement microsite */
#speakers, #academic-honors {
    max-width: 1100px;
    margin: auto;
}

#speakers h2, #academic-honors h2, #global-celebrations h2 {
    line-height: 1.2;
}

#speakers .is-infobox {
    max-width: 870px !important;
}

@media screen and (max-width: 781px) {
    #speakers .is-speaker + .is-speaker {
        margin-top: 48px;
    }
}

@media screen and (max-width: 900px) {
    .commencement-2024-header .banner svg {
        max-width: 80vw;
    }

    #academic-honors .is-infobox .wp-block-media-text__content {
        padding-top: 16px;
    }

    #global-celebrations .wp-block-columns {
        padding-left: 8px;
        padding-right: 8px;
    }
}

.page-id-223749 .slick-slide .tiktok-embed {
    max-width: 320px !important;
}

/** commencement article blocks */

#highlights .wp-block-post-template {
    display: grid;
    gap: 18px 16px;
    grid-template-columns: repeat(3, 1fr);
    justify-content: space-between;
}

#highlights .is-alternate-layout {
    grid-template-columns: repeat(3, 1fr);
}


#highlights .wp-block-post-template .wp-block-post-title {
    padding: 16px 8px;
}

#highlights :where(body .is-layout-flow) > * {
    margin-block-start: 0;
}

#highlights .featured-articles.has-spread-view > div:first-of-type {
    display: block
}

/** commencement articles grid 4:n */
@media screen and (max-width: 959px) {
    .has-hidden-mobile-images .post-thumbnail {
        display: none;
    }
}

@media screen and (min-width: 960px) {
    #highlights .featured-articles.has-spread-view {
        max-width: var(--wp--custom--layout--extra-extra-wide-size) !important;
    }

    /* #highlights .featured-articles.has-spread-view  > div:first-of-type {
        display: grid;
        grid-column-gap: calc(2rem + 1px);
        grid-row-gap: 8px;
        grid-template-columns:repeat(15, 1fr);
        grid-template-rows: repeat(2, 1fr);
    } */
    #highlights .featured-articles.has-spread-view > div:first-of-type > article {
        grid-column: span 6;
        width: 100%;

    }

    #highlights .featured-articles.has-spread-view > div:first-of-type > article .entry-title {
        font-size: var(--wp--custom--font-size--large) !important;
    }

    #highlights .featured-articles.has-spread-view > div:first-of-type > article:first-of-type {
        grid-column: 1/ span 11;
        grid-row: 1/  -1;
    }

    #highlights .featured-articles.has-spread-view > div:first-of-type > article:first-of-type .entry-title {
        font-size: var(--wp--custom--font-size--x-large) !important;

    }

    #highlights .featured-articles.has-spread-view > div:first-of-type > article:nth-of-type(2) {
        grid-row: 1;
        grid-column: 12/ span 4
    }

    #highlights .featured-articles.has-spread-view > div:first-of-type > article:nth-of-type(3) {
        grid-row: 2;
        grid-column: 12 / span 4
    }
}

#highlights .wp-block-post-template {
    display: grid;
    gap: 18px 16px;
    grid-template-columns: repeat(4, 1fr);
    justify-content: space-between;
}

#highlights .is-alternate-layout {
    grid-template-columns: repeat(3, 1fr);
    display: flex;
}


#highlights .wp-block-post-template .wp-block-post-title {
    padding: 16px 8px;
}

#highlights :where(body .is-layout-flow) > * {
    margin-block-start: 0;
}

#highlights .is-alternate-layout > div {
    display: flex;
    flex-flow: row;
    flex-wrap: nowrap;
    flex-wrap: wrap;
    gap: 0 16px;
    justify-content: space-between;
}

#highlights .is-alternate-layout {
    margin-top: 32px;
    border-top: 1px #ddd solid;
    padding-top: 32px;
}


#highlights .is-alternate-layout .post-thumbnail {
    width: 70px;
    height: 70px;
    flex-basis: 70px;
    overflow: hidden;
    flex-shrink: 0;
}

#highlights .is-alternate-layout .post-thumbnail img {
    object-fit: cover;
    width: 100%;
    height: 100%;
}

#highlights .is-alternate-layout .wp-block-post-title {
    padding: 0
}

#highlights .is-alternate-layout article {
    display: flex;
    margin-bottom: 12px;
}

#highlights .is-alternate-layout .entry-title {
    font-size: var(--wp--custom--font-size--medium) !important;
}

#highlights .is-alternate-layout .entry-wrapper {
    padding: 0 4px 4px 16px;
    flex: 1;
}

/** Commencement articles */
@media screen and (max-width: 959px) {
    .has-hidden-mobile-images .post-thumbnail {
        display: none;
    }
}

@media screen and (min-width: 960px) {
    #highlights .featured-articles.has-spread-view {
        max-width: var(--wp--custom--layout--extra-extra-wide-size) !important;
        display: grid;
        grid-column-gap: calc(2rem + 1px);
        grid-row-gap: 8px;
        grid-template-columns:repeat(15, 1fr);
    }


    #highlights .spread-row-1-2 {
        grid-column: 1 / span 11;
    }

    #highlights .spread-col-justify {
        grid-column: 12 / span 4;
        margin: 0;

    }

    #highlights .spread-col-justify > div {
        display: flex;
        flex-direction: column;
        height: 100%;
    }

    #highlights .spread-col-justify > div > * {
        flex: 1;
        width: 100%;
    }

    #highlights .spread-col-justify .entry-title {
        font-size: var(--wp--custom--font-size--medium) !important;
    }

        grid-column-gap: calc(2rem + 1px);
        grid-row-gap: 8px;
        grid-template-columns:repeat(15, 1fr);
        grid-template-rows: repeat(2, 1fr);
    }

    #highlights .has-spread-view article {
        width: 100%;
    }

    #highlights .spread-row-1-2 article .entry-title {
        font-size: var(--wp--custom--font-size--large) !important;

    }

    .spread-row-1-2 > div > article:nth-child(1) {
        grid-column: 1/ span 7;
        grid-row: 1/  -1;
    }

        flex-direction: row-reverse;
        grid-row: 1;
        grid-column: 8/ span 8;
    }

    .spread-row-1-2 > div:first-of-type > article:nth-of-type(3) {
        display: flex;
        flex-direction: row-reverse;
        grid-row: 2;
        grid-column: 8 / span 8;
    }
}

<body class="home blog wp-theme-ngn-theme">
<style>.ngn-bar.is-announcement.is-empty { display: none; }
.ngn-bar.is-announcement .marquee-viewport {
	overflow: hidden;
	width: 100%;
	display: flex;
	justify-content: flex-start;
}
@media (min-width: 600px) {
	.ngn-bar.is-announcement .marquee-viewport { justify-content: center; }
}
.ngn-bar.is-announcement.is-marquee .marquee-viewport { justify-content: flex-start; }
.ngn-bar.is-announcement.is-centered .marquee-viewport { justify-content: center; }
.ngn-bar.is-announcement .marquee-track {
	display: inline-flex;
	white-space: nowrap;
	flex-shrink: 0;
}
.ngn-bar.is-announcement.is-marquee .marquee-track { will-change: transform; }
.ngn-bar.is-announcement.is-marquee .marquee-viewport {
	cursor: grab;
	touch-action: pan-y;
	user-select: none;
}
.ngn-bar.is-announcement.is-dragging .marquee-viewport { cursor: grabbing; }</style><script>window.__ngnAnnouncementContext = {"post_id":0,"post_type":"","post_subtree_slugs":[]};</script><div class="ngn-bar is-size-dense has-surface-color-scheme is-sticky-top is-announcement is-empty" aria-label="Site announcement" role="status"><div class="bar__wrapper is-flex is-items-center should-fill-height is-relative is-justify-center alignregion"><div class="marquee-viewport"><div class="marquee-track"><span class="marquee-content is-family-sans is-weight-normal is-size-13 has-line-height-16 has-letter-spacing-slight"></span></div></div></div></div>
<script>(function () {
	var POLL_INTERVAL_MS = 30000;
	var ENDPOINT         = '/wp-json/ngn/announcements';
	var SCHEME_CLASSES   = ['has-primary-color-scheme', 'has-surface-color-scheme'];
	var SPEED_PX_PER_SEC = 25;
	var END_HOLD_MS      = 2000;
	var DRAG_THRESHOLD   = 5;

	var REDUCE_MOTION = window.matchMedia &&
		window.matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches;

	var bar, viewport, track, content;
	var currentId    = null;
	var marqueeStop  = null;   // teardown function for the active marquee, if any
	var ctx          = window.__ngnAnnouncementContext || {
		post_id: 0, post_type: '', post_subtree_slugs: []
	};

	function isVisible(msg) {
		var v = msg && msg.visibility ? msg.visibility : {};
		if (Array.isArray(v.only_post_types) && v.only_post_types.length &&
			v.only_post_types.indexOf(ctx.post_type) === -1) return false;
		if (Array.isArray(v.exclude_post_ids) &&
			v.exclude_post_ids.indexOf(ctx.post_id) !== -1) return false;
		if (Array.isArray(v.exclude_post_subtree_slugs)) {
			for (var i = 0; i < v.exclude_post_subtree_slugs.length; i++) {
				if (ctx.post_subtree_slugs.indexOf(v.exclude_post_subtree_slugs[i]) !== -1) {
					return false;
				}
			}
		}
		return true;
	}

	function urgencyToScheme(urgency) {
		return urgency === 'urgent'
			? 'has-primary-color-scheme'
			: 'has-surface-color-scheme';
	}

	function applyScheme(name) {
		for (var i = 0; i < SCHEME_CLASSES.length; i++) {
			bar.classList.remove(SCHEME_CLASSES[i]);
		}
		bar.classList.add(name);
	}

	function teardownMarquee() {
		if (marqueeStop) { marqueeStop(); marqueeStop = null; }
		bar.classList.remove('is-marquee', 'is-centered', 'is-dragging');
		track.style.transform = '';
		bar.style.removeProperty('--ngn-marquee-duration');
		bar.style.removeProperty('--ngn-marquee-distance');
	}

	function setupMarquee() {
		var overflow = content.scrollWidth - viewport.clientWidth;
		if (overflow <= 0) {
			bar.classList.add('is-centered');
			return;
		}

		bar.classList.add('is-marquee');

		// Anchors are draggable by default; disable so our pointer
		// logic owns the gesture instead of the browser's text DnD.
		bar.querySelectorAll('a').forEach(function (a) {
			a.setAttribute('draggable', 'false');
		});

		var pos = 0, direction = -1, lastFrame = null;
		var holdUntil = performance.now() + END_HOLD_MS;
		var hovering = false, dragging = false, pendingDrag = false;
		var dragStartX = 0, dragStartPos = 0;
		var rafId = 0;
		var suppressNextClick = false;

		function apply() { track.style.transform = 'translateX(' + pos + 'px)'; }
		apply();

		function step(now) {
			if (REDUCE_MOTION || hovering || dragging) {
				lastFrame = now; rafId = requestAnimationFrame(step); return;
			}
			if (now < holdUntil) {
				lastFrame = now; rafId = requestAnimationFrame(step); return;
			}
			var dt = lastFrame ? (now - lastFrame) / 1000 : 0;
			lastFrame = now;
			pos += direction * SPEED_PX_PER_SEC * dt;
			if (direction === -1 && pos <= -overflow) {
				pos = -overflow; direction = 1; holdUntil = now + END_HOLD_MS;
			} else if (direction === 1 && pos >= 0) {
				pos = 0; direction = -1; holdUntil = now + END_HOLD_MS;
			}
			apply();
			rafId = requestAnimationFrame(step);
		}
		rafId = requestAnimationFrame(step);

		function onMouseEnter()  { hovering = true; }
		function onMouseLeave()  { hovering = false; lastFrame = null; }
		function onDragStart(e)  { e.preventDefault(); }

		function startPointer(x) { pendingDrag = true; dragStartX = x; dragStartPos = pos; }
		function movePointer(x) {
			if (!pendingDrag && !dragging) return;
			var d = x - dragStartX;
			if (!dragging && Math.abs(d) >= DRAG_THRESHOLD) {
				dragging = true; bar.classList.add('is-dragging');
			}
			if (dragging) {
				pos = Math.max(-overflow, Math.min(0, dragStartPos + d));
				apply();
			}
		}
		function endPointer() {
			if (dragging) {
				suppressNextClick = true;
				bar.classList.remove('is-dragging');
				holdUntil = performance.now() + END_HOLD_MS;
			}
			dragging = false; pendingDrag = false; lastFrame = null;
		}

		function onMouseDown(e)  { if (e.button === 0) { e.preventDefault(); startPointer(e.clientX); } }
		function onMouseMoveDoc(e) { movePointer(e.clientX); }
		function onMouseUpDoc()  { endPointer(); }
		function onTouchStart(e) { if (e.touches.length === 1) startPointer(e.touches[0].clientX); }
		function onTouchMove(e)  { if (e.touches.length === 1) movePointer(e.touches[0].clientX); }
		function onTouchEnd()    { endPointer(); }
		function onClick(e) {
			if (suppressNextClick) {
				e.preventDefault(); e.stopPropagation();
				suppressNextClick = false;
			}
		}

		bar.addEventListener('mouseenter', onMouseEnter);
		bar.addEventListener('mouseleave', onMouseLeave);
		bar.addEventListener('dragstart',  onDragStart);
		bar.addEventListener('mousedown',  onMouseDown);
		document.addEventListener('mousemove', onMouseMoveDoc);
		document.addEventListener('mouseup',   onMouseUpDoc);
		bar.addEventListener('touchstart',  onTouchStart, { passive: true });
		bar.addEventListener('touchmove',   onTouchMove,  { passive: true });
		bar.addEventListener('touchend',    onTouchEnd,   { passive: true });
		bar.addEventListener('touchcancel', onTouchEnd,   { passive: true });
		bar.addEventListener('click', onClick, true);

		marqueeStop = function () {
			cancelAnimationFrame(rafId);
			bar.removeEventListener('mouseenter', onMouseEnter);
			bar.removeEventListener('mouseleave', onMouseLeave);
			bar.removeEventListener('dragstart',  onDragStart);
			bar.removeEventListener('mousedown',  onMouseDown);
			document.removeEventListener('mousemove', onMouseMoveDoc);
			document.removeEventListener('mouseup',   onMouseUpDoc);
			bar.removeEventListener('touchstart',  onTouchStart);
			bar.removeEventListener('touchmove',   onTouchMove);
			bar.removeEventListener('touchend',    onTouchEnd);
			bar.removeEventListener('touchcancel', onTouchEnd);
			bar.removeEventListener('click', onClick, true);
		};
	}

	function showMessage(msg) {
		if (msg.id === currentId) {
			// Same message; nothing to do.
			return;
		}
		teardownMarquee();
		content.innerHTML = msg.html;
		applyScheme(urgencyToScheme(msg.urgency));
		bar.classList.remove('is-empty');
		currentId = msg.id;
		// Defer marquee setup until layout has settled with the new
		// content (the just-set innerHTML hasn't been measured yet).
		requestAnimationFrame(setupMarquee);
	}

	function hide() {
		if (currentId === null) return;
		teardownMarquee();
		content.innerHTML = '';
		bar.classList.add('is-empty');
		currentId = null;
	}

	function applyResponse(messages) {
		if (!Array.isArray(messages)) return;
		for (var i = 0; i < messages.length; i++) {
			if (isVisible(messages[i])) { showMessage(messages[i]); return; }
		}
		hide();
	}

	function tick() {
		if (document.visibilityState !== 'visible') return;
		fetch(ENDPOINT)
			.then(function (r) { return r.ok ? r.json() : null; })
			.then(function (data) { if (data) applyResponse(data.messages); })
			.catch(function () {});
	}

	function init() {
		bar      = document.querySelector('.ngn-bar.is-announcement');
		if (!bar) return;
		viewport = bar.querySelector('.marquee-viewport');
		track    = bar.querySelector('.marquee-track');
		content  = bar.querySelector('.marquee-content');
		if (!viewport || !track || !content) return;

		tick();
		setInterval(tick, POLL_INTERVAL_MS);
		document.addEventListener('visibilitychange', function () {
			if (document.visibilityState === 'visible') tick();
		});
	}

	if (document.readyState === 'loading') {
		document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', init);
	} else {
		init();
	}
})();</script>	<!-- Google Tag Manager (noscript) -->

					  height="0" width="0" style="display:none;visibility:hidden"></iframe></noscript>

<header class="site-header">

	<h2 class="is-family-display-condensed is-weight-medium is-size-21 has-line-height-24 has-letter-spacing-moderate">
		Is the &ldquo;work from home&rdquo; era over in Boston? Here&rsquo;s why companies want employees back in the office.	</h2>
</div><div class="ngn-card__body has-grow-on has-pt-base has-pb-base has-px-compact">	<div class="is-family-sans is-weight-light is-size-15 has-line-height-24 has-letter-spacing-comfortable">
		One other financial incentive? Avoiding layoffs, according to Northeastern Management &amp; Organizational Development Professor Jayanth Narayanan. A forced return to work might inspire some employees to quit and look for work elsewhere.&nbsp;&ldquo;Then you say, &lsquo;Oh, you know what? That&rsquo;s fewer number of people that I have to lay off, right?'&rdquo; he said. &ldquo;Maybe 5-10% of [&hellip;]	</div>
</div></a>
</div><div class="layout__cell is-flex is-flex-col has-basis-full medium:is-col-span-8 large:is-col-span-6"><a class="ngn-card has-surface-color-scheme has-border-variant has-surface-color-scheme is-flex has-grow-off is-interactive is-direction-col is-fit-height" href="https://www.newsweek.com/term-limits-congress-popular-whats-stopping-them-11925287" target="_blank"><div class="ngn-card__headline has-pt-compact has-px-compact">	<div class="is-flex is-items-center has-mb-component" style="height: 16px;width: 120px">
					<img width="626" height="108" src="https://news.northeastern.edu/wp-content/uploads/2025/09/newsweek-logo.png?w=626" class="attachment-small size-small" alt="Newsweek logo" style="height: 100%;object-fit: contain;object-position: left center;width: 100%" decoding="async" srcset="https://news.northeastern.edu/wp-content/uploads/2025/09/newsweek-logo.png 626w, https://news.northeastern.edu/wp-content/uploads/2025/09/newsweek-logo.png?resize=196,34 196w, https://news.northeastern.edu/wp-content/uploads/2025/09/newsweek-logo.png?resize=335,58 335w" sizes="(max-width: 626px) 100vw, 626px">			</div>
	<h2 class="is-family-display-condensed is-weight-medium is-size-21 has-line-height-24 has-letter-spacing-moderate">
		Term Limits for Congress Are Overwhelmingly Popular&mdash;What&rsquo;s Stopping Them?	</h2>
</div><div class="ngn-card__body has-grow-on has-pt-base has-pb-base has-px-compact">	<div class="is-family-sans is-weight-light is-size-15 has-line-height-24 has-letter-spacing-comfortable">
		Daniel Urman, a&nbsp;law&nbsp;professor at Northeastern University, told&nbsp;Newsweek&nbsp;on Thursday that the ruling means neither Congress nor state legislatures can &ldquo;add qualifications for being in Congress beyond those already in the Constitution.&rdquo;	</div>
</div></a>

<script type="text/javascript" id="ngn-embed-lazyload-js-after">
/* <![CDATA[ */
(function () {
	var LAZY_HOSTS = ['instagram.com', 'tiktok.com'];
	var PLACEHOLDER = 'about:blank';
	var ROOT_MARGIN = '400px';

	function isLazyHost(url) {
		try {
			var host = new URL(url, location.href).host;
			return LAZY_HOSTS.some(function (h) { return host.indexOf(h) !== -1; });
		} catch (e) {
			return false;
		}
	}

	function lazify(iframe, doc) {
		if (iframe.dataset.ngnLazified) return;

		// Server-prepared path: PHP filter already swapped src to
		// about:blank and stashed the real URL in data-ngn-src. No
		// browser preload-scanner race here; we just observe and
		// restore on intersection.
		var serverPrepared = !!iframe.dataset.ngnSrc;
		var originalSrc;

		if (serverPrepared) {
			originalSrc = iframe.dataset.ngnSrc;
		} else {
			// Runtime fallback for iframes that landed in the DOM
			// without server prep (editor canvas, dynamically
			// inserted blocks). Fights the preload-scanner race —
			// the iframe may still have already started fetching —
			// but cancelling further loads is better than nothing.
			var src = iframe.getAttribute('src');
			if (!src || src === PLACEHOLDER || !isLazyHost(src)) return;
			originalSrc = src;
			iframe.dataset.ngnSrc = originalSrc;
			iframe.setAttribute('src', PLACEHOLDER);
		}

		iframe.dataset.ngnLazified = '1';
		if (!iframe.getAttribute('loading')) {
			iframe.setAttribute('loading', 'lazy');
		}

		var view = doc.defaultView || window;
		if (!view.IntersectionObserver) {
			iframe.setAttribute('src', originalSrc);
			return;
		}
		var io = new view.IntersectionObserver(function (entries) {
			entries.forEach(function (entry) {
				if (entry.isIntersecting) {
					iframe.setAttribute('src', originalSrc);
					io.disconnect();
				}
			});
		}, { rootMargin: ROOT_MARGIN });
		io.observe(iframe);
	}

	function scan(doc) {
		var iframes = doc.querySelectorAll('iframe');
		for (var i = 0; i < iframes.length; i++) {
			lazify(iframes[i], doc);
		}
	}

	function watch(doc) {
		if (doc.__ngnLazyWatched) return;
		doc.__ngnLazyWatched = true;
		scan(doc);
		var view = doc.defaultView || window;
		if (!view.MutationObserver) return;
		var mo = new view.MutationObserver(function (mutations) {
			for (var i = 0; i < mutations.length; i++) {
				var added = mutations[i].addedNodes;
				for (var j = 0; j < added.length; j++) {
					var node = added[j];
					if (node.nodeType !== 1) continue;
					if (node.tagName === 'IFRAME') {
						lazify(node, doc);
					} else if (node.querySelectorAll) {
						var nested = node.querySelectorAll('iframe');
						for (var k = 0; k < nested.length; k++) {
							lazify(nested[k], doc);
						}
					}
				}
			}
		});
		mo.observe(doc.body || doc.documentElement, { childList: true, subtree: true });
	}

	function attachToCanvas() {
		var canvas = document.querySelector('iframe[name="editor-canvas"]');
		if (!canvas) return false;
		var hook = function () {
			var doc = canvas.contentDocument;
			if (doc && doc.body) watch(doc);
		};
		hook();
		canvas.addEventListener('load', hook);
		return true;
	}

	function boot() {
		watch(document);
		if (!attachToCanvas()) {
			var tries = 0;
			var iv = setInterval(function () {
				if (attachToCanvas() || ++tries > 40) clearInterval(iv);
			}, 250);
		}
	}

	if (document.readyState === 'loading') {
		document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', boot);
	} else {
		boot();
	}
})();
